It is the top of that list, and I sampled it for exactly that reason.  *** WHAT IS THERE, AND IT IS A CLASS THIS PROJECT HAS ALREADY FIXED ELSEWHERE: ***  the guard at the head of the CRIME_RE_TLB_A arm is  `if (len != 8) { fatal(\"TODO: unimplemented len=%i ...\"); exit(1); }`  -- **a GUEST CHOOSING AN ACCESS WIDTH ends the host process.**  That is the `#337` class verbatim, whose own comment in `dev_wdc.c` reads *'a 3- or 8-byte access to the data port used to call exit(1) -- a GUEST choosing an access width killed the host process'*.  The adjacent arm kills on a plain READ (`fatal(\"TODO: read from CRIME_RE_TLB_A\"); exit(1);`), which is the direction/offset class.  **REACHABILITY CHECKED, NOT ASSUMED**: `machine_sgi.c:500` calls `dev_sgi_re_init(machine, mem, 0x15000000)` and the device registers at `baseaddr + 0x1000` through `memory_device_register`.  It is a real device on a real machine, mapped into the guest address space.  **HONEST BOUND ON THE NUMBER, because this project keeps getting counts wrong: 21 is how many live terminating calls sit inside that file's `DEVICE_ACCESS` handlers.**  It is NOT a claim that 21 are individually reachable -- some may be guarded, and `dev_dreamcast_gdrom.c:1055` is the standing proof that a `DEVICE_ACCESS` site can be unreachable and take a hand-derived warrant to establish it.  What IS established: at least two of them are reachable by construction, and they are of classes already recognised as defects here.  *** THE GENERAL LESSON, AND IT IS ABOUT THE QUEUE RATHER THAN THIS DEVICE: A QUEUE-COMPLETE CLAIM IS NOT A TREE-COMPLETE ONE. ***  The adjudicating seat flagged exactly this limit about its own answer hours earlier -- *'my no-other-live-host-kills claim rests on the queue and this file's grep, not a fresh tree-wide exit census'* -- and then I wrote the queue-complete version into a brief as a premise anyway.  The seat named the gap; the brief inherited it.  SCOPE: this is a CLUSTER and must not become one round.  The round-scope rule takes ONE site, with a second joining only if it is witnessed, in the same device and semantic class, and covered by the same detector with no new oracle.  The width guards may qualify as one class; the read-direction kills are a second.  **More witnesses create more candidate ROUNDS, never bigger rounds.**  NEXT STEP is a rung-3 witness on ONE site, not a fix: `-E sgi` machines exist in tree, the device is mapped at a known base, and the `#337` precedent gives the fix shape (latched decline, `(once)` marker) if a witness lands.  *** THE DIAGNOSIS ABOVE IS INCOMPLETE AND THE TRUE ONE IS WORSE.  Corrected the same day by the opus5 seat, which MEASURED it. ***  This row was filed saying the brief had confused 'the queue knows of no others' with 'there are no others'.  **Neither.  The queue knew of NINE others.**  Ledger row `n6` is OPEN, titled *'The record says the footbridge host-halts were made graceful; nine of them are not'*, carrying a CONFIRMED rung-3 witness (`probe:regress/footbridge_sites_probe.py` row K1, gated in `gate_arm.sh`) and a `state_note` RANKING IT 6th OF 8 on 2026-08-27 -- three days before the brief claimed there were none.  The seat rebuilt HEAD clean and re-ran the COMMITTED probe: a guest `ldr r7,[r0,#392]` on an unmodified `-E cats` ends the host at `dev_footbridge.c:589`.  It then swapped the single planted word three more times and got **four of four** -- `:604` ENABLE_CLEAR read, `:565` IRQ_STATUS write, `:712` SDRAM_BA_MASK read -- each one guest instruction, each with the site's own diagnostic text as the attribution control.  **So this is the UNREAD-ROW class, not a census gap.**  The census work still stands and still found two REAL clusters nobody had filed; what it did not do is what a `grep n6` would have done in one second.  *The lesson is not 'census harder'.  It is that a completeness claim must be checked against THE QUEUE'S OWN OPEN ROWS before it is written down -- and that is the cheaper check of the two.*  *** THE CENSUS IS DONE, AND IT SPLITS THE 21 INTO FOUR CLASSES WITH ONE PROVABLY DEAD SITE.  agy seat, 2026-08-30, `regress/artifacts/widthkillclass/witness_20260830/`. ***  It is a READING, not a measurement, and is recorded as one -- but the single most surprising claim in it was CHECKED HERE BY OPENING THE FILE, and it held.  **20 of 21 guest-reachable; 1 PROVABLY UNREACHABLE.**  WIDTH (a guest choosing an access size ends the host) -- 7 reachable: the `len != 8` guards on CRIME_RE_TLB_A/B/C, CRIME_RE_TEX and CRIME_RE_LINEAR_A/B, plus `sgi_de: len = %i not implemented` (which wants len 4 or 8).  DIRECTION (a legal-width access in the wrong direction) -- 6 reachable: `TODO: read from CRIME_RE_TLB_A/B/C`, `..._TEX`, `..._LINEAR_A/B`, each reached by an 8-byte READ, i.e. **the guest does everything right except the direction**.  BADOFFSET -- 1: the `unimplemented read/write to address` arm, reached by any access to an unregistered sub-window.  BADVAL -- 6: `sgi_de: UNIMPLEMENTED drawing op`, and five `sgi_mte` arms (MTE_MODE_STIPPLE, unimplemented SRC, MTE_MODE_COPY, MTE_DEPTH_x, and an unimplemented bytemask).  *** THE UNREACHABLE ONE IS DEAD CODE AND MUST NOT BE 'FIXED' -- I VERIFIED THIS BY OPENING THE FILE RATHER THAN ACCEPTING IT. ***  `[ sgi_mte: UNIMPLEMENTED read/write len %i ]` sits in the trailing `else` of `if (len == 4) { ... } else if (len != 4) { ... } else { fatal; exit(1); }`.  For every integer `len` one of the first two arms is taken, so the third can never execute.  A round that 'de-fatalises' it would be editing unreachable code and its detector could carry no row -- **the vacuous shape in its purest form, and the census is what stops it being attempted.**  **WHAT THIS MEANS FOR SCOPE, and it is the useful part: FOUR classes means at least four candidate rounds, not one.**  The round-scope rule takes ONE site, with a second joining only if it is the same device AND the same semantic class AND covered by the same detector with no new oracle.  A WIDTH guard and a DIRECTION guard sit in the SAME ARM at the same offset and are still different classes -- whether they nonetheless share one fix design is an open question this row does not settle, and it is exactly what the next research pass should answer rather than assume.  HONEST BOUND: reachability here is derived from address-decode reading, NOT measured, except for the WIDTH class -- which now has a rung-3 witness (see `widthkillclass`).  The DIRECTION, BADOFFSET and BADVAL classes have NO witness yet and a round on any of them owes one first.  *** THE COUNT'S SCOPE WAS WRONG AND A SEAT CHECKED IT: 21 IS THE FILE TOTAL, NOT THE HANDLER'S. ***  `DEVICE_ACCESS(sgi_re)` holds **13**.  Per handler: sgi_re 13, sgi_de 1, sgi_mte 1, sgi_de_status 0, helpers 6 -- summing to the 21 this row filed.  So the four-class census spans FOUR HANDLERS, not one, and that matters for scope rather than for tidiness: a round joining sites 'in the same handler' is a different population from one joining sites 'in the same file'.  **This is the dropped-qualifier class again** -- the number was right and the noun was wrong, which is exactly how `b120r`'s NIL and `#464`'s 'floods identically' went wrong.  AND THE WIDTH FIGURE OF 7 IS SHORT BY ONE against the message text: there are EIGHT width-shaped kills in the file, the six in sgi_re plus `sgi_de: len = %i not implemented` and `[ sgi_mte: UNIMPLEMENTED read/write len %i ]`. ignored' about a read that is implemented and is not ignored.*  **REACHABILITY: all six are reachable by an ordinary guest load on unmodified `-E sgi -e ip32`**, and the addresses are derived rather than guessed -- `machine_sgi.c:500` passes base `0x15000000`, `dev_sgi_re_init` registers `sgi_re` at `baseaddr + 0x1000` for `DEV_SGI_RE_LENGTH` (0x1000), and the handler restores the full offset with `relative_addr += 0x1000`.  **Every window lies inside `0x15001000-0x150017ff`, none is shadowed, and the `else if` cascade is strictly disjoint.**  Verified here independently by opening `machine_sgi.c` and `crmfbreg.h`: the six constants are `0x1000 / 0x1200 / 0x1400 / 0x1600 / 0x1700 / 0x1780`.  **GUEST KSEG1 ADDRESSES, so a probe author does not re-derive them:** TLB_A `0xb5001000`, TLB_B `0xb5001200`, TLB_C `0xb5001400`, TEX `0xb5001600`, LINEAR_A `0xb5001700`, LINEAR_B `0xb5001780` -- read kills at lines 245, 262, 279, 296, 346, 362 respectively.  **One malloc serves all four handlers** (`dev_sgi_re_init` allocates one `struct sgi_re_data` and hands it to `sgi_de`, `sgi_mte` and `sgi_de_status`), so whatever latch infrastructure `#497` lands is already reachable here.  *The audit and the brief that produced it are preserved at `regress/artifacts/sgirekills/audit_20260830/` -- the ledger first cited the `_scratchpad/` copy, which the durable-path rule excludes precisely because it evaporates.  This row now has everything a rung-3 witness needs except the witness itself, which is the point of asking a seat for the addresses rather than the answer.*  *** THIS ROW'S TITLE SAID '21 TERMINATING CALLS INSIDE DEVICE_ACCESS' AND THAT WAS THE SAME CONFLATION I MADE AND CORRECTED ELSEWHERE TODAY.  Corrected 2026-08-30. ***  **21 is the FILE total.  15 are inside a `DEVICE_ACCESS`, and 13 of those in `DEVICE_ACCESS(sgi_re)`** -- the other 6 live in `draw_primitive` (1) and `do_mte_transfer` (5), neither of which is a `DEVICE_ACCESS` and neither of which a guest reaches by an ordinary access.  *** AND THE TITLE WAS NOT CARELESS -- IT FAITHFULLY REPORTED WHAT THE TOOL SAID, WHICH WAS WRONG. ***  See `exitcensus`: `exit_census.py`'s per-file totals were correct all along, but its BY-ENCLOSING-CONSTRUCT split attributed every call in a plain non-static function to the preceding `DEVICE_ACCESS`.  That bucket is a REACHABILITY claim used for ranking -- *on a guest access path BY CONSTRUCTION* -- so the defect over-stated how many host kills a guest can actually reach.  Fixed; tree-wide the bucket falls **97 -> 83**.  *** THE ACCEPT DESIGN, ADJUDICATED 2026-08-30 -- AND IT REFUTES THE AUDIT RECORDED DIRECTLY ABOVE IT ON ONE ARM OF SIX. ***  **agy ruled ACCEPT for all six read arms on the ground that BACKING STATE EXISTS in the struct.  That is not the ACCEPT test.**  The doctrine's fourth option-set member -- minted from the footbridge IRQEnable precedent -- is *the model already computes the CORRECT answer and merely discards it*.  Existence of state is necessary and nowhere near sufficient.  **FIVE ARMS ARE CLEAN ACCEPT; TEX IS NOT.**  **TLB A/B/C:** the store packs big-endian halfword pairs from raw bus bytes and a readback through the same index expression round-trips BYTE-EXACTLY on this machine -- with a premise that belongs in the comment rather than assumed: `machine_sgi.c` pins BIG ENDIAN, and while the STORE path is byte-order-agnostic the READ path is not.  **LINEAR A/B:** the store splits `idata` into two uint32 cells and the readback is its exact inverse through the `readmax64`/`writemax64` pair -- byte-order symmetric BY CONSTRUCTION, so it needs no endianness premise at all.  *** TEX FAILS THE TEST, AND THE EVIDENCE IS A GREP: `re_tex` HAS ZERO CONSUMERS ANYWHERE. ***  `tlb_a/b/c` feed `horrible_getputpixel`; `linear_a/b` feed it and `do_mte_transfer`; **`re_tex` feeds nothing.**  Its state is written through the `>> 3` index that `sgitexindex` records as a defect -- frames overlap by three, 81 of 112 cells unreachable -- so **the model does not hold the right answer for TEX; it holds a self-consistent WRONG one.**  ACCEPT is available only in the MIRRORED form (the read uses the same `>> 3` expression), which round-trips per frame while shipping hardware-wrong reads at overlapping offsets.  **That is a materially weaker claim than the other five arms', and the round record must not blur them.**  **WHAT ACCEPT OWES THAT DECLINE DOES NOT -- five items, and the second is an ORDERING DEPENDENCY on `#497`:**  (1) **round-trip rows with DISTINCT sentinels per window** -- distinctness catches a cross-wired read, while a same-window index error breaks round-trip because the store frame is position-dependent.  The JOINT translation (store and read shifted together) escapes round-trip BY CONSTRUCTION -- clause (f) territory -- so the contract names it outside, and names honestly that an out-of-decode anchor channel exists for four arms (the rendering and MTE paths consume them) and **does not exist for `re_tex` today**.  (2) **a DEVICE-SIGNATURE control**, because a faithful round-trip through a device is value-indistinguishable from RAM -- the witness ladder's own two-control lesson.  The clean discriminator: **post-`#497` a 4-byte read DECLINES and returns 0 while RAM would serve it.**  So `#497` is an ordering dependency for this round's WITNESS QUALITY, not merely for its latch infrastructure.  (3) **the dead tail's first execution belongs to `#497`, not here.**  A width-declined READ falls through the splice and runs `memory_writemax64` with `odata = 0`.  This round's new surface is narrower than it looks: **NONZERO `odata` through the BE packing**, not 'the tail runs'.  (4) **the claim must be SCOPED**: *returns what the store path recorded*, never *faithful hardware readback*.  TEX refutes the stronger phrase today, and the LINEAR arms store the PROM's own wrongly-sign-extended values verbatim -- returning them faithfully is right; modelling hardware readback is unproven.  **That is `b716a34`'s lesson pre-applied: do not put the strong phrase in a site comment.**  (5) **a PRE-WRITE read row** returning 0, which pins the memset-init as guest-visible ABI and is the minimal de-fatalisation survival row.  *** TEX POISONS THE WARRANT, NOT THE ROUND -- AND THE COUPLING IS ENFORCEABLE BY ONE ROW RATHER THAN BY A DEPENDENCY. ***  Ship TEX MIRRORED plus its round-trip row, and any ONE-SIDED future fix reds that row loudly: store `>> 1` against read `>> 3` writes cells 4-7 and reads 1-4, and the reverse likewise, while the JOINT fix keeps identity and the row stays green.  **So the round-trip row IS the coupling enforcement**, and `sgitexindex`'s fix becomes a declared two-site edit with a tripwire already in place.  Recorded in BOTH rows.  **WHAT IS NOT ACCEPTABLE:** unmirrored `>> 1` reads over the `>> 3` store -- round-trip broken immediately, silent wrong data, worse than a decline -- or ACCEPT recorded with the same warrant strength as the other five arms.  **A LATENT THIS ROUND INHERITS RATHER THAN CREATES:** the TLB arms mask the INITIAL `tlbi` but increment it UNMASKED in the loop, so `relative_addr & 0x1ff >= 0x1fa` with `len 8` runs to index 0x102 -- three halfwords into the adjacent array.  Guest-unreachable for the alignment reason `sgilinearoob` settles path-by-path, and **a mirrored read arm makes it a second SITE of that class** -- record it there so an eventual bound fixes both directions.  *** THE TEST THAT KILLED `sgideoob` WAS THEN APPLIED TO THE SURVIVORS, BY ENUMERATION RATHER THAN BY REASONING -- AND BOTH HOLD.  2026-08-30. ***  Withdrawing a false member is only half the follow-through; the other half is asking whether the same miss is in the rows that remain.  The refuting method for `sgideoob` was *what is this index's ACTUAL RANGE?* -- so every reachable `relative_addr` in each window was walked and the resulting index recorded.  **TLB LOOP** (`re_tlb_a[256]`, `tlbi = ((ra & 0x1ff) >> 1) & 0xff`, four writes at `len 8`): with 8-byte ALIGNMENT -- the only thing a guest can produce -- the maximum index written is **255, IN BOUNDS**.  Unaligned, reachable only by the debugger's `put d`, it is **258, OOB by three halfwords**.  *Exactly as recorded: guest-unreachable, debugger-reachable, same disposition as `sgilinearoob`.*  **`sgitexindex`** (`re_tex[112]`, `tlbi = ((ra & 0xff) >> 3) & 0xff`, window `0x1600-0x16df`): maximum index written is **30, comfortably IN BOUNDS of [112]** -- so this row is NOT an out-of-bounds and never claimed to be.  Its three stated figures were each re-derived independently and all match: **81 cells unreachable**, **3 entries of overlap** between consecutive 8-byte writes.  *** SO THE CLASS AFTER THE WITHDRAWAL IS: TWO REAL OOBs (`sgilinearoob`, the TLB loop), BOTH GUEST-SHIELDED BY ALIGNMENT AND BOTH DEBUGGER-REACHABLE; ONE NON-OOB CORRECTNESS DEFECT (`sgitexindex`); AND ONE WITHDRAWN FALSE MEMBER. ***  Every one now tested by the same method, which is what the false member's whole lesson was.  *** THE WITNESS FIELD WAS STALE AND SAID THE OPPOSITE OF WHAT SHIPPED. THIS ROW'S OWN TITLE WAS WRONG, MY BRIEF CONTRADICTED ITSELF, AND BOTH ARE NOW SETTLED FROM THE SOURCE. ***  **(1) THE CENSUS.  The title said each register *accepts exactly ONE value*; that is true of ONE arm out of four.**  Derived, with the last constant looked up after glm named it as the settling fact:  `W_MASK0` accepts exactly `{0x700000}` -- one.  `W_BASE0` accepts `{0, 0x300800000}` -- **two**.  `IOC_CONF` kills iff `idata & ~1`, so it accepts `{0, 1}` -- **two**, and the predicate is *bit 0 only*, not a constant.  `TB_ENA` kills iff `idata & ~IOC_TB_ENA_TEN`, and `alpha_lcareg.h` defines `IOC_TB_ENA_TEN 0x0000000000000080ULL` -- a SINGLE bit, so it accepts `{0, 0x80}` -- **two**.  Title corrected.  *This matters beyond tidiness: the de-escalation rule owes an accept row per predicate, and a detector built from `exactly one value` would pin the wrong accept side on three of the four arms.*  **glm's sharpest point is about WHICH value to pin:** for `W_BASE0` the accepted `0` is the *maximally ordinary* write -- zeroing a window -- so an accept row pinned to the exotic magic constant *misfires on the most legal write rather than an unusual one*.  **(2) MY BRIEF CONTRADICTED ITSELF AND THE SOURCE SETTLES IT AGAINST ME.**  Q2 asserted these fields *feed address-window decoding*; Q3's note said the emulator *does not otherwise model them*.  glm caught that both cannot be true and asked which.  Measured (`grep -n 'window_base_0|window_mask_0|tlb_enable|ioc_conf' src/devices/dev_lca.c`): `window_base_0`, `window_mask_0` and `tlb_enable` are **written and read back and nothing else** -- each appears exactly twice, as `odata = d->X` and `d->X = idata`.  **Only `ioc_conf` is CONSUMED**, at `bus = d->ioc_conf & 1`.  *** SO THE FIX DESIGN SPLITS, AND IT WOULD NOT HAVE WITHOUT THIS CHECK. ***  Three of the four arms are pure BACKED ACCEPT storage -- a decline that keeps the store harms nothing, because no emulator code reads the field.  **The fourth is different**: `ioc_conf`'s low bit selects the bus, and its guard is `idata & ~1`, so a rejected value's bit 0 still reaches the consumer.  glm's *\"a decline that keeps the store is an accept with a complaint, so validate BEFORE storing and leave the previous value\"* is right **for that arm specifically**, and is over-general for the other three.  **(3) `idata` IS `uint64_t`** (`memory_readmax64`), so `0x300800000` is representable and glm's width worry is answered -- the comparison is reachable, not dead code.  **(4) Reachability stays a READING, correctly.**  All three seats declined to say whether these are the only values REAL HARDWARE accepts or merely the only ones this emulator was developed against, and read the code's own wording (`TODO`, *\"differs from the expected values\"*) as supporting the second.  There is no LCA datasheet in `_scratchpad/`.  *That is the honest form and it is flagged as a reading rather than a fact.*\n\n*** MAIN-LOOP MEASUREMENT 2026-08-31 (not a seat answer; recorded here rather than in `entries` so it cannot be counted toward the panel). ***  evidence: dev_lca.c :203 :222 :237 :252 :276 :291 (six guards); :129 inside a comment block\n\n*** THREE WRONG RECORDS ON THIS ROW, found by the rung-3 seat and RE-VERIFIED BY READING dev_lca.c before being written here. ***\n\n(1) SCOPE: there are SIX value-guarded arms, not four. Likewise, merely checking that issued and delivered counts agree permits the empty result `0 == 0`; the witness requires a positive issuance assertion.

GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/codex_fixdesign_20260911.txt:74: Several blind spots are already provable:
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/codex_fixdesign_20260911.txt:75: 
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/codex_fixdesign_20260911.txt:76: - **Twelve one-byte writes starting at `A` cannot distinguish lengths 1 and 2.** Both mappings deliver identical accesses. An exact extent assertion�?"or an independent boundary check�?"is necessary to detect that width mutation.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/codex_fixdesign_20260911.txt:77: - **Direct calls to the auxiliary handler cannot test the missing machine case.** They bypass initialization and address dispatch, so removing the case cannot change their result.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/codex_fixdesign_20260911.txt:78: - **The primary handler�?Ts non-byte-access warning is not an auxiliary-handler oracle.** The warning belongs to `DEVICE_ACCESS(wdc)`. The auxiliary callback has no corresponding length guard, and all reported lengths are one anyway. [dev_wdc.c:609](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_wdc.c:609), [dev_wdc.c:1087](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_wdc.c:1087)

GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/codex_fixdesign_20260911.txt:80: Counting resets is also insufficient to count delivery: only writes containing `WDCTL_RST` enter the reset action, and repeated resets can leave identical state. The twelve payload values are not supplied. The control branch itself is the appropriate delivery observation point. [dev_wdc.c:740](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_wdc.c:740)

[dev_wdc.c:2028](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_wdc.c:2028)
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/codex_fixdesign_20260911.txt:94: 
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/codex_fixdesign_20260911.txt:95: For these offset-zero, one-byte accesses, narrowing preserves delivery. The auxiliary handler does **not** divide its offset by `addr_mult`; the primary two-byte stride does not require a two-byte auxiliary registration. Narrowing additionally removes auxiliary ownership of `A + 1`, which the twelve reported writes cannot test.

GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:92: 
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:93: WHAT MAPPING THE REGISTER WOULD THEN DO (read from DEVICE_ACCESS(wdc_altstatus)):
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:94:   * on the 0x0c write, idata & WDCTL_RST is true, so the handler sets
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:95:     d->cur_command = COMMAND_RESET and calls wdc_new_command_reset(), which
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:96:     clears atapi_cmd_in_progress, atapi_phase and write_in_progress and purges
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:97:     the inbuf ring (inbuf_head = inbuf_tail = 0).  The guard is LEVEL-triggered
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:98:     by deliberate #481 design.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:99:   * the six 0x08 and five 0x02 writes reach no WDCTL_RST branch; they would
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:100:     produce only a debug() line, invisible without -v.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:101:   * reads would return status_byte(), which is PURE (verified by reading it: it
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:102:     derives from diskimage_exist, the ring pointers, write_in_progress, d->error
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:103:     and the atapi phase, and assigns nothing).  Today they return 0 instead --
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:104:     but the guest performs ZERO reads there in this boot, so that half is not
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:105:     observable on this rig.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:106:   * cur_command = COMMAND_RESET (0x100) is a LATCH.  Its only two consumers are
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:107:     the cyl_lo and cyl_hi read arms, and BOTH are gated on
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:108:     diskimage_is_a_cdrom(), so on this IDE-disk rig they do not fire.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:109: 
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:110:   NOT SETTLED: whether delivering the reset changes the boot outcome, and what a
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:111:   CD-ROM rig (-d ic:) would do -- both consumers of the latch are cdrom-gated and
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:112:   this census used a plain IDE disk.  Those are fix-side measurements the round
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:113:   still owes; this census does not answer them.

GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:181: and ZERO "write to ALT. CTRL" for the whole boot.  So today the handler that
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:182: exists is never reached, and the twelve writes that should reach it are discarded
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:183: 0x1da bytes lower down.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:184: 
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:185: ================================================================================
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:186: A TRAP WORTH RECORDING -- IT PRODUCED A CLEAN, CONFIDENT, HALF-EMPTY CENSUS
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:187: ================================================================================
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:188: The first version of the census hook tested its window on paddr at do_return_ok.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:189: It reported 12 unmapped writes and ZERO command-block accesses, with no error and
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:190: no missing denominator -- and that was wrong.  The device arm of
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:191: MEMORY_RW_ONE_PAGE does "paddr -= mem->devices[i].baseaddr" BEFORE calling the
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:192: handler, so a device access reaches do_return_ok carrying the RELATIVE offset
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:193: (0x00..0x0f), never 0x14000000+.  A window predicate written on paddr is
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:194: therefore blind to exactly the mapped half -- which is the half that answers Q3.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:195: The fix is the GXC_P0 latch, taken immediately after address translation.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:196: 
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:197: *** THIS IS PRECISELY HOW A REPORT COULD COME BACK WITH THE UNMAPPED WRITES AND
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:198: NO COMMAND-BLOCK CONTROL AT ALL.  A censusing instrument must be shown to see the
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:199: POSITIVE case before its negatives are believed; here the 2560/2560 DATA
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:200: agreement against the tree's own -v output is that proof. ***
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:201: 
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/landiskaux/census_20260827/RESULTS.txt:202: ================================================================================

GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:37:              This is the ACCEPT control the de-escalation clause demands: it proves the
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:38:              fix leg does not disable the register it is supposed to leave alone, and it
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:39:              proves the reset machinery this probe observes is alive.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:40: 
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:41:     PHASE 2  poke the SAME 0x06 at 0x3f7, the READ-ONLY Drive Address Register.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:42:              *** THE DEFECT.  Committed build: identical to phase 1.  Fix leg: nothing
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:43:              happens. ***
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:44: 
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:45:     PHASE 3  poke 0x02 (nIEN alone, WDCTL_RST clear) at 0x3f7.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:46:              Neither build resets.  This is the discriminator that says the defect is
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:47:              the RESET BIT reaching a register that has none, rather than "any store to
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:48:              0x3f7 does something".
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:49: 
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:50: PHASE 1 AND PHASE 2 ARE THE ANCHOR (vacuity-floor clause (f)).  They are the same
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:51: stimulus at ADJACENT addresses, so the pair pins WHERE the store lands, not merely that
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:52: two addresses behave differently.  On the committed build the two phases are
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:53: indistinguishable -- which IS the defect, stated as a measurement.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:54: 
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:55: THE TWO CONTROLS THE WITNESS LADDER DEMANDS FOR AN ACCESS WITNESS:
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:56: 
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:57:     LIVENESS          three of them.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:58:                       (a) RAM: the same sb/lbu instruction shapes round-trip 0x55
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:59:                           through plain RAM, so a device row reading 0x00 could not be
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:60:                           blamed on a bad encoding.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:61:                       (b) DEVICE REGISTER: 0x55 -> cyl_lo and 0x66 -> cyl_hi read back
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:62:                           as 0x6655 through the wdc's own decode.  This is #481's
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:63:                           preserved liveness=0x6655 idiom, kept deliberately so this
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:64:                           witness and that one are comparable.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:65:                       (c) per phase: the ARMED status read, which must show DRQ before
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:66:                           the poke or the phase measured nothing.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:67: 
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:68:     DEVICE SIGNATURE  two of them.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:69:                       (a) FIFO: push 0x5a then 0xa5 to the data port and read the SAME
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:70:                           address twice.  A memory cell returns 0xa5 both times; only a
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:71:                           FIFO returns them in push order.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:72:                       (b) STATUS: the byte at +7 is COMPUTED by status_byte() out of
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:73:                           diskimage_exist(), d->error and d->write_in_progress.  The
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:74:                           guest never wrote it.  RAM would answer with what was stored.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:75: 
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:76: WHY THIS IS ALSO A DETECTOR, NOT ONLY A WITNESS.  Every row carries a want for BOTH
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:77: legs.  Phase 2's two rows are the only ones whose want DIFFERS between the legs; every
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:78: other row is an identity row that must hold on both.  So the file fails on the committed
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:79: build, passes once the handler decodes its offset, and still fails if a future fix
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:80: over-reaches: a build that refuses offset 0 as well reds PHASE 1, and a build that keeps
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:81: the decode but nulls its effect reds PHASE 2 again.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:82: 
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:83: BYTE ORDER.  evbmips -e malta is LITTLE-endian.  Every access here is a single byte, so
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:84: byte order cannot silently change what a row means -- stated because a sibling detector
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:85: in this tree was measured testing the wrong endianness.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:86: 
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:87: Usage:  wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py <base-binary> <fix-binary> <disk-image> [outdir]
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/wdcaltoff_rung3_probe.py:88:

GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/RESULTS.txt:69:   The data port was read TWICE at the SAME address and returned 0x5a then 0xa5.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/RESULTS.txt:70:   A memory cell returns the last value written (0xa5) both times; only a FIFO
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/RESULTS.txt:71:   returns them in push order.  This is the wdc input ring, not a memory word.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/RESULTS.txt:72:   The status byte at +7 read 0x51 at power-on and 0x58 armed.  DRDY|DSC = 0x50
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/RESULTS.txt:73:   is computed by status_byte() out of diskimage_exist(), the ERR bit out of the
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/RESULTS.txt:74:   d->error that dev_wdc_init sets, and DRQ out of d->write_in_progress.  The
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/RESULTS.txt:75:   guest wrote none of those three, and RAM would return what was stored.
GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/RESULTS.txt:76:   RAM liveness (0x55) and register liveness (0x6655 -- #481's 0x6655 idiom)
